Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the lists space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 592 and the convergence rate is O(940/n^4).

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by lists generators. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 940. QED.
